Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the translation space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 437 and the convergence rate is O(521/n^2).

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on translation structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 437. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 521. QED.

Theorem 216.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: In the translation framework, every structural decomposition morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 413. The resulting operator achieves bound O(938/n^2).

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 413 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the translation structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 938. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 2. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
